#376999 12 2007-11-05T00:45:25Z Pepi
Quote: chaosraider, November 04, 2007 09:34 pm
NEVER EVER EVER use tiles from the n - game in episodes.

its unoriginal

Yes, never do that.

You need to remember that the tileset is half of your map! No matter how good your object placement is, the map will be bad if the tileset is, and vice versa.

A very brief summary of how to not make noob maps:
-don't spam objects like mines and gold
-NEVER use launchpad chains
-always playtest your level
-try to make it enjoyable and fun throughout (this comes with experience)

The fourth map was second best, but there were just too many drones.

#409814 22 2008-02-15T23:42:07Z Pepi
Quote: Snooks, 2008-02-15T23:28:05Z
So your saying we need weak stupid people to feed us McDonalds? And you accused me of creating a "beta" class?

Ok lets get some things straight. Firstly, I never accused you of creating a beta class. What I meant was that it would be necessary to have one under your ridiculous system. I am not saying we need that class to give us McDonalds food. But would an educated person, growing up with access to money and power, having been genetically engineered, want to do the boring jobs? Of course not.

Quote: Snooks, 2008-02-15T23:28:05Z
I have been a member of the military for the past six years and have many friends who have spent over 20 years in service. Your belittling us all to say that if people were all smart no one would do the job I do. People serve in the military for a huge number of reasons from the training they offer to the competitive pay or to see the world. Some people, I have met them, choose to join the military because they are idealists and they fight for their country.

I did not intend to belittle the brave soldiers of the military. If you and your friends have actually served in the military for years, then I applaud you. You are probably more than a grunt soldier. Doesn't the better off person aspire to rank higher? Would an army of manipulated, smart people get anything done? No. Why? Because the military is tough work... it's dangerous (duh) and you don't have access to very many luxuries.

I am not opposed to making the human race "healthy and smart" if, and only if, basic rights are not violated. Like aborting babies because they don't make the cut. Like changing the DNA of someone because it is not standard. You do that, and you take all uniqueness out of the world. You will create just a bunch of machines that look like humans.

And also, how will you define an "undesirable characteristic"? Who decides what is desirable and what isn't? It smells fishy.

Here's what I'm trying to say: everybody has a place in society. They are in that place because they deserve to be. The poor, highschool drop-out that works at McDonalds is there because he didn't try to get an education. If you cut out the lower class, which is what you're suggesting, then you will cut out the middle class, who will in turn cut out the upper class. The upper class cannot survive without the lower classes. Basically, what you suggest, if taken to an extreme, would destroy humanity.

Civil War: Americans have hundreds of thousands of troops, with hundreds of thousands of deaths. Which war is bigger? Which war had more American casualties than any other war in American history?

If it were, I would totally agree that Washington was better. But we aren't... we're looking at their presidencies.

I think that we'll have to agree to disagree here.

#413110 6 2008-03-01T18:37:27Z Pepi
You mean to add levels to your userlevels? Use this: $levelname#authorname#comments#leveldata#
For example, $Test Level#skipper1993#hard#RAW LEVEL DATA HERE#
Alternatively, you can use NUMA (N Users Map Archive) to store your maps.
#413119 11 2008-03-01T19:14:26Z Pepi
Add them into your "userlevels" file that came with N when you downloaded it. Further instructions are inside it. Be sure that it is in the same location on your computer as N, otherwise it won't work. Then click on "userlevels" from the main menu. Try NUMA, though. It was created for that purpose.
#413124 13 2008-03-01T19:23:42Z Pepi
Yes, under the "configure" menu of N, you can create your account. Remember to submit only the raw data to NUMA, not the whole $author# thing. Feel free to PM myself or any mod if you have further questions. Also, consult this thread.

#415432 2 2008-03-09T23:04:16Z Pepi
WWII was caused by neither science nor religion. The seeds of it were planted when the Treaty of Versailles was signed. This treaty came into effect after WWI when Germany had lost, and it forced Germany to not only pay war reparations to the Allies in full, but to accept all responsibility for the entirety of WWI. The broken German economy was obviously unable to pay the debt, and it caused a lot of hatred among the Germans towards the Allies. A political figure rose to power, vowing revenge. His name was Adolf Hitler. He supported eugenics, he wanted territory for Germany, and ultimately, world domination.

The Treaty of Versailles "fell between two stools" as the saying goes. There were two options available to the Allies: make a generous, forgiving armistice, or exact incredibly harsh punishment that completely destroyed the German military and occupied/governed Germany for a long time. Versailles did neither and both at the same time. It allowed Germany to keep a small army, didn't occupy Germany, and forced reparations. The combination of hatred and a military were the cause of World War Two.

I think those facts are right... I apologize if any are incorrect.

#19463 1 2008-03-10T23:05:47Z Pepi
I searched for this but didn't come up with anything, so here goes.

I believe that a loose interpretation is necessary/best for our system of government. Basically, a loose interpretation is something is allowed if the Constitution doesn't say you cannot do it. The strict version is its opposite: you can only do what the Constitution says you can do and nothing else. (By this I mean legislation, not personal rights)

Thoughts? Arguments?

#418199 4 2008-03-18T02:58:20Z Pepi
Then at any low rating, the map maker could simply just go and snipe other people's maps. Honestly, this would get tedious. Some maps just don't deserve a comment. Also, I think that you're overestimating the sniping problem. As far as I've seen, and I've been on NUMA a lot recently, there really has been little or no sniping. Your most recent map, eN or whatever, didn't get sniped. I think it just got voted what people thought that it deserved... just because someone thought it good enough for a size, that's totally subjective. Other people don't have to share that opinion. "Sniping" is when somebody rhythmically goes through all of a members levels and zero's them. If this happens to you, just email or PM 'nid and he'll take care of it for ya. I think you're overestimating this problem. Well, either that or it has just subsided for a while.

Actually, as some members would argue, sniping is a good thing. (yeah, sounds crazy, right?) I don't really know the logic behind this, but after I post this I'll hunt around for some stuff.

#418556 4 2008-03-18T22:54:12Z Pepi
Hi there, and welcome to the forums.
Firstly, please use NUMA to share your maps. NUMA stands for N Users Map Archive. To register for an account, use the "configure" menu inside N. Next, the Map Rating Thread and the Map Commenting Thread are useful ways to get feedback on your levels without having to make a new topic each time. As for the maps, post them on NUMA and I'll look at them ASAP.

If you have any questions about the forum or NUMA, feel free to PM myself or any of the moderators.

Enjoy your stay here!

I have bitesizing privileges. I would guess that there are around 200 sizers, of which 75-100 are still active.

Oh, and the more sizers there are does NOT mean more bitesized maps. Sizers are (supposed to be) trustworthy, and don't give unfair sizes. The amount of worthy maps won't change with the number of people able to size them.
#420553 11 2008-03-23T18:17:44Z Pepi
Think about it. There are 317 people with an average rating of four or more, and to size you don't even need to have a four. Look through the random stats. It makes sense. Of course, sizers are not chosen on their statistics. Most of the choice is based on the six maps you say you would size. Usually, if you make good maps you have better judgment. This guy has sizing privileges, and his average rating was 2.5 when he got them. If I remember correctly, he had about 100 maps with maybe 10 rated, and got sizing rights.

Yeah, 20 is way on the low side. I could probably name off 20 right now.

#427872 54 2008-04-13T02:11:17Z Pepi
Sky Panda. Do you make maps because you enjoy doing it, or do you make maps to receive good ratings? Personally, I make maps because I enjoy it. Ratings are just a tasty appetizer. Sometimes the chef forgets to cook the meat - but you still have a good meal, for an appetizer does not compromise the entire dinner. What you can do is to not even eat that appetizer in the first place, instead of yelling at the chef telling him to cook your meat more.

Seriously, we all play this game for enjoyment. Entertainment. It isn't something to break a leg over - and also isn't worth screaming at the chef.

Ok, enough metaphors. What this boils down to is, is get over it and deal with it. End of story.
